const C=CHALLENGE,SID=SESSION,$=id=>document.getElementById(id);
let points=[],elapsed=0,started=null,closed=false;
const roundAway=x=>{const n=Math.trunc(x),f=x-n;return n+(f>=.5?1:f<=-.5?-1:0);};
function geometry(u){const q=C.curve;if(C.motion==='arc'&&q){const a=1-u,b=[a*a*a,3*a*a*u,3*a*u*u,u*u*u];return {x:b[0]*q.start_x+b[1]*q.control1_x+b[2]*q.control2_x+b[3]*q.end_x,y:b[0]*q.start_y+b[1]*q.control1_y+b[2]*q.control2_y+b[3]*q.end_y};}return {x:u*C.max_x,y:C.piece_y};}
function draw(){const p=geometry(Number($('slider').value)/10000),scale=$('stage').clientWidth/C.width;$('piece').style.width=C.size*scale+'px';$('piece').style.transform=`translate(${p.x*scale}px,${p.y*scale}px)`;}
function record(force){if(started===null||closed)return;const u=Number($('slider').value)/10000,p=geometry(u),next={x:roundAway(u*C.max_x),y:roundAway(p.y),t:Math.floor(elapsed+performance.now()-started)},last=points[points.length-1];if(!force&&last&&last.x===next.x&&last.y===next.y)return;points.push(next);if(points.length>96){let best=Infinity,index=1;for(let i=1;i<points.length-1;i++){const a=points[i-1],b=points[i],c=points[i+1],dt=c.t-a.t,w=dt>0?(b.t-a.t)/dt:.5,dx=b.x-(a.x+(c.x-a.x)*w),dy=b.y-(a.y+(c.y-a.y)*w),score=dx*dx+dy*dy+((b.x-a.x)*(c.x-b.x)<0?1e9:0);if(score<best){best=score;index=i;}}points.splice(index,1);}$('status').textContent='已记录 '+points.length+' 个操作点';}
function begin(e){if(!e.isTrusted||started!==null||closed)return;started=performance.now();$('submit').disabled=true;record(false);}
function end(e){if(!e.isTrusted||started===null)return;record(true);elapsed+=performance.now()-started;started=null;const last=points[points.length-1];$('submit').disabled=closed||points.length<2||!last||last.x<=1;}
$('slider').addEventListener('pointerdown',begin);window.addEventListener('pointerup',end);
$('slider').addEventListener('keydown',e=>{if(['ArrowLeft','ArrowRight','ArrowUp','ArrowDown','Home','End','PageUp','PageDown'].includes(e.key))begin(e);});$('slider').addEventListener('keyup',end);
$('slider').addEventListener('input',e=>{draw();if(e.isTrusted)record(false);});
$('slider').addEventListener('pointercancel',()=>{if(started!==null){elapsed+=performance.now()-started;started=null;}$('submit').disabled=true;});
async function finish(action){if(closed)return;if(action==='CONFIRM'&&(started!==null||points.length<2||points[points.length-1].x<=1))return;closed=true;for(const el of document.querySelectorAll('button,input'))el.disabled=true;const answer={action,scope:C.scope,challenge_id:C.challenge_id};if(action==='CONFIRM'){answer.x=points[points.length-1].x;answer.points=points;}const raw=JSON.stringify(answer);try{if(window.FanShuInput){window.FanShuInput.submit(raw);}else{const r=await fetch('/proxy?do=config&action=push&sid='+encodeURIComponent(SID)+'&key=slider_result&value='+encodeURIComponent(raw),{cache:'no-store'});if(!r.ok)throw Error('transport');const data=await r.json();if(data.success!==true)throw Error('expired');}$('status').textContent='已回传，请在设备上查看验证结果';}catch(e){$('status').textContent='回传未确认，请在设备上重新打开验证';}}
$('submit').onclick=()=>finish('CONFIRM');$('refresh').onclick=()=>finish('REFRESH');$('cancel').onclick=()=>finish('CANCEL');
